    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;

mb-14" data-page-title="Function Calling" data-page-href="/guides/capabilities/function-calling" id="content"><span data-as="p">Function Calling allows AI models to call external functions and APIs, greatly expanding the capability boundaries of intelligent agents, enabling them to perform specific operations and obtain real-time data.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Function calling provides AI models with the ability to interact with external systems, supporting various complex application scenarios and integration requirements.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="core-parameter-description"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#core-parameter-description" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Core Parameter Description</span></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>tools</code></strong>: Defines the list of callable functions, including function names, descriptions, and parameter specifications</li>
<li><strong><code>tool_choice</code></strong>: Controls function calling strategy, default is <code>auto</code> (only supports <code>auto</code>)</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="response-parameter-description"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#response-parameter-description" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Response Parameter Description</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Key fields in function calling responses:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>tool_calls</code></strong>: Contains information about functions the model decides to call</li>
<li><strong><code>function.name</code></strong>: Name of the called function</li>
<li><strong><code>function.arguments</code></strong>: Function call parameters (JSON format string)</li>
<li><strong><code>id</code></strong>: Unique identifier for the tool call</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="code-examples"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#code-examples" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Code Examples</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">By defining function tools and handling function calls, AI models can perform various external operations:</span>
<div class="tabs tabs tab-container"><ul role="tablist" class="not-prose mb-6 pb-[1px] flex-none min-w-full overflow-auto border-b border-gray-200 gap-x-6 flex dark:border-gray-200/10" data-testid="tab-Python SDK"> <!-- -->Python SDK</div></li></ul><div><div id="panel-python-sdk-0" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="python-sdk" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0!" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Install SDK</strong></span><div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl <span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">from</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> zai </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">import</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> ZaiClient</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Initialize client</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">client </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> ZaiClient(</span><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">api_key</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&#x27;your_api_key&#x27;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Define weather query function</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">def</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> get_weather</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">city</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">str</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">) -&gt; </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">dict</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;&quot;&quot;Get weather information for specified city&quot;&quot;&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">    # This should call a real weather API</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    weather_data </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;city&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: city,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;temperature&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;22°C&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;condition&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Sunny&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;humidity&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;65%&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;wind_speed&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;5 km/h&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">    return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> weather_data</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Define function tools</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">tools </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;function&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;function&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;name&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;get_weather&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Get current weather information for specified city&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;parameters&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;object&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;properties&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;city&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                        &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                        &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;City name, e.g.: Beijing, Shanghai&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;required&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;city&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Make conversation request</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">response </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> client.chat.completions.create(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    model</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;glm-5.3&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,  </span><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Use model that supports function calling</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    messages</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">[</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        {</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;role&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;user&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;content&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;How&#x27;s the weather in Beijing today?&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    tools</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">tools,         </span><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Pass function tools</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    tool_choice</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;auto&quot;</span><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">   # Automatically choose whether to call functions</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Handle function calls</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">message </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> response.choices[</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">0</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">].message</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">messages </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> [{</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;role&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;user&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;content&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;How&#x27;s the weather in Beijing today?&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">messages.append(message.model_dump())</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">if</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> message.tool_calls:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">    for</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> tool_call </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">in</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> message.tool_calls:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        if</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> tool_call.function.name </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">==</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;get_weather&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">            # Parse parameters and call function</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            args </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> json.loads(tool_call.function.arguments)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            weather_result </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> get_weather(args.get(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;city&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">            # Return function result to model</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            messages.append({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;role&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;tool&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;content&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: json.dumps(weather_result, </span><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">ensure_ascii</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">False</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;tool_call_id&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: tool_call.id</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">    # Get final answer</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    final_response </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> client.chat.completions.create(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        model</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;glm-5.3&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        messages</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">messages,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        tools</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">tools</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    )</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">    print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(final_response.choices[</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">0</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">].message.content)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">else</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">    print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(message.content)</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div></div>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">    def</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> calculate</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">expression</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">str</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">):</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;&quot;&quot;Safe mathematical calculation&quot;&quot;&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        try</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">            # Simple security check</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            allowed_chars </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0"> set</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&#x27;0123456789+-*/().&#x27;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            if</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> not</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> all</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(c </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">in</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> allowed_chars </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">or</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> c.isspace() </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">for</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> c </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">in</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> expression):</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">                return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;error&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Expression contains disallowed characters&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            result </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> eval</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(expression)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;expression&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: expression,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;result&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: result</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">    def</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> execute_function</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">function_name</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">str</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">arguments</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">dict</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">):</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;&quot;&quot;Execute function call&quot;&quot;&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        if</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> function_name </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">==</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;get_current_time&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.get_current_time()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        elif</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> function_name </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">==</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;calculate&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.calculate(arguments.get(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;expression&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        elif</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> function_name </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">==</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;search_web&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.search_web(arguments.get(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;query&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        else</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;error&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">f</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Unknown function: </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">{</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">function_name</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">    def</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> chat</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">user_message</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">str</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">):</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;&quot;&quot;Handle user message&quot;&quot;&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        messages </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> [{</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;role&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;user&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;content&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: user_message}]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        response </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.client.chat.completions.create(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">            model</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;glm-5.3&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">            messages</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">messages,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">            tools</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.tools,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">            tool_choice</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;auto&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        )</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        message </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> response.choices[</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">0</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">].message</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        messages.append(message.model_dump())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">        # Handle function calls</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        if</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> message.tool_calls:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            for</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> tool_call </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">in</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> message.tool_calls:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                function_name </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> tool_call.function.name</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                arguments </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> json.loads(tool_call.function.arguments)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">                # Execute function</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                result </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.execute_function(function_name, arguments)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">                # Add function result</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                messages.append({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;role&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;tool&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;content&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: json.dumps(result, </span><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">ensure_ascii</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">False</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;tool_call_id&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: tool_call.id</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">            # Get final answer</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            final_response </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.client.chat.completions.create(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">                model</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;glm-5.3&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">                messages</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">messages,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">                tools</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">self</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.tools</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            )</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> final_response.choices[</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">0</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">].message.content</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        else</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">            return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> message.content</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Usage example</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">agent </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> FunctionAgent(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;your_api_key&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Test different types of requests</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(agent.chat(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;What time is it now?&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(agent.chat(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Help me calculate 15 * 23 + 7&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(agent.chat(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Search for the latest developments in artificial intelligence&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">))</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></details>

**:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-2xl bg-white"><div role="presentation" data-id="base-ui-_R_dbkllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" style="background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="python"><code language="python" numberOfLines="40"><span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">import</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> sqlite3</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">def</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA"> query_database</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">sql</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">str</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">) -&gt; </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">dict</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;&quot;&quot;Execute database query&quot;&quot;&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">    try</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        conn </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> sqlite3.connect(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&#x27;example.db&#x27;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        cursor </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> conn.cursor()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        cursor.execute(sql)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        results </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> cursor.fetchall()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        conn.close()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        </span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;success&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">True</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;data&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: results,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;row_count&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">len</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(results)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">    except</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0"> Exception</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0"> as</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> e:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;success&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">False</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;error&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4EC9B0">str</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(e)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Function definition</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">db_tool </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;function&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;function&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;name&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;query_database&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Execute SQL query&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;parameters&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;object&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;properties&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;sql&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;SQL query statement&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;required&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;sql&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></details>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Function definition</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;properties&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;operation&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;enum&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;read&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;write&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;list&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Operation type&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;file_path&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;File path&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;content&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Content to write (only required for write operation)&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;required&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;operation&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;file_path&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></details>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;enum&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;GET&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;POST&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;HTTP method&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;headers&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;object&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Request headers&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                &quot;data&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;object&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">                    &quot;description&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Request data&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">            },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">            &quot;required&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;url&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></details>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="best-practices"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#best-practices" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Best Practices</span></h2>
<div class="columns prose dark:prose-invert grid max-w-none gap-4 @2xl/columns-container:grid-cols-[repeat(var(--cols),minmax(0,1fr))] @[0px]/columns-container:grid-cols-1 card-group dark:prose-dark gap-y-0 sm:grid-cols-[repeat(var(--cols),minmax(0,1fr))]!" style="--cols:2"><div class="card block font-normal group relative my-2 ring-2 ring-transparent rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-background-dark border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 overflow-hidden w-full"><div class="px-6 py-5 relative" data-component-part="card-content-container"><div aria-hidden="true" class="size-6 fill-gray-800 dark:fill-gray-100 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-6" data-component-part="card-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! size-6 m-0! shrink-0 bg-primary dark:bg-primary-light" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/code.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/code.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="w-full"><h2 id="_R_ckllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_" class="not-prose font-semibold text-base text-gray-800 dark:text-white mt-4" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="card-title">Function Design Principles</h2><div id="_R_ckllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutbH1_" class="prose mt-1 font-normal text-base leading-6 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400" data-component-part="card-content"><ul>
<li>Single responsibility: Each function should do one thing</li>
<li>Clear naming: Function and parameter names should be meaningful</li>
<li>Complete description: Provide detailed function and parameter descriptions</li>
</ul></div></div></div></div><div class="card block font-normal group relative my-2 ring-2 ring-transparent rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-background-dark border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 overflow-hidden w-full"><div class="px-6 py-5 relative" data-component-part="card-content-container"><div aria-hidden="true" class="size-6 fill-gray-800 dark:fill-gray-100 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-6" data-component-part="card-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! size-6 m-0! shrink-0 bg-primary dark:bg-primary-light" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/shield.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/shield.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="w-full"><h2 id="_R_kkllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_" class="not-prose font-semibold text-base text-gray-800 dark:text-white mt-4" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="card-title">Security Considerations</h2><div id="_R_kkllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutbH1_" class="prose mt-1 font-normal text-base leading-6 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400" data-component-part="card-content"><ul>
<li>Input validation: Strictly validate all input parameters</li>
<li>Permission control: Limit function access permissions</li>
<li>Logging: Record function call logs</li>
</ul></div></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="parameter-design"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#parameter-design" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Parameter Design</span></h3>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="error-handling"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#error-handling" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Error Handling</span></h3>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">        return</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;error&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Input contains dangerous keywords&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
